# Bathymetry of Oceania and the Pacific: Australia (Commonwealth, states, territories, external territories), New Zealand and its realm, the Pacific island states and territories, regional compilations, lakes, reservoirs and rivers
Run date 2026-11-13 (board item i1322, task #1322). Research only. Method: four area sub-agents (A: Australia's Commonwealth products and the eastern states with the ACT; B: Western Australia, South Australia, the Northern Territory, the external territories and Australian inland waters; C: New Zealand and its realm; D: the Pacific island states and territories, regional compilations, cruise grids and deep-sea minerals), each given the exact table columns of bathy-africa-me.md and a number block (A 1-99, B 100-199, C 200-299, D 300-399, so numbers skip and are stable). A second pass (PM review) added rows 400-412, 450-462 and 500-515, rewrote the cells of 26 earlier rows to the corrected facts, and folded its notes into sections 3, 5 and 8 under 'Second pass'. I checked every merged row for the 12-column shape (two rows of D had stray pipes inside a URL and an extra cell; fixed), read each agent's headline and the table rows' product, spacing, licence and index cells, and wrote section 1 from the finished tables. Fetches identify as `scenic-roads/0.1 (personal offline map)`; data files that decided a fact were downloaded and read (headers, indexes, metadata, PDFs); other sizes come from HEAD requests and publisher metadata. Pointers: GEBCO_2026, its TID grid and GMRT are in bathy-global.md rows 1-2; Hawaii, Guam, CNMI and American Samoa (PacIOOS, CUDEM, NCEI DEMs) in bathy-namerica.md section 2.6 and 2.1; Easter Island in bathy-samerica.md. Column order of every table: product and version | publisher | kind | spacing | measured or interpolated and where the source/coverage index lives | vertical datum | year | coverage | format and access | licence and personal offline cache | footprint or index URL.

## 1. Headline

- Australia has one current open national grid with a measured-vs-predicted map: GA AusBathyTopo 250 m 2026 (published 2026-08-04, CC BY 4.0, MSL depth EPSG:5715, 2.2 GB zip with a GEBCO-style TID grid and a source list of 1,542 sources; Heard/McDonald and Antarctica excluded; rows 1-4). Finer open national layers on files.ausseabed.gov.au, all CC BY 4.0: the 50 m multibeam compilation 2018 (5.6 GB, measured only), Great Barrier Reef 30 m (four overlapping grids) and 100 m, Torres Strait, Northern Australia and Bass Strait 30 m, Kerguelen/Macquarie grids, and the IMAS multi-resolution composite of the whole EEZ (10 m to 200 m depth, 32/64/128/210 m deeper; CC BY 4.0; unzipped packages exceed 100 GB).
- Measured-coverage index for Australia: the AusSeabed Warehouse GeoServer (https://warehouse.ausseabed.gov.au/geoserver/wfs), anonymous, with 3,295 survey acquisitions and 61 compilations, each row carrying a licence field and a data URL (2,042 CC BY 4.0, 1,247 "Restricted", a few others) and 560 per-product coverage layers; the IMAS composite has its own source-footprint WFS (1,183 features). GA's services.ga.gov.au answered 403.
- States and territories with their own data: Western Australia is the richest and fully open: the Department of Transport's 29 five-metre composite surfaces (Perth, Rottnest, Geraldton, Albany, Esperance, Broome, Dampier, Exmouth and others) and 43 single-beam zips, all CC BY 4.0 (rows 101-104). The Northern Territory has the 1 m Darwin and Bynoe Harbour multibeam compilation, CC BY 4.0 (rows 130-131). NSW has a statewide mosaic of the 2018 marine lidar (5 m) plus Seabed NSW multibeam listed "cc-by", whose SEED/Planning Portal downloads sit behind a WAF challenge (not bypassed); the same OEH multibeam archive (67 survey GeoTIFFs, 2005-2018, 5 m bins, AHD, CC BY 4.0) is open and anonymous on AODN (row 450), and Sydney Harbour 2 m (2004) and 16 m (1997), Jervis Bay and Shellharbour grids are open on files.ausseabed.gov.au (rows 451-454). Victoria's VCDEM 2021 (2.5 m and 10 m, CC BY 4.0) is orderable only through a DEECA Datashare login. Queensland has the Sunshine Coast lidar (5 m), Gold Coast City surveys, Maritime Safety Queensland soundings (points, depths below LAT) and a Moreton Bay satellite-derived grid (3 m, 2022-23, CC BY 4.0, row 457); port bathymetry for Gladstone, Townsville, Cairns and Mackay was not found; Tasmania has IMAS SeaMap contours and single-beam, Storm Bay 5 m multibeam; South Australia and the ACT have no state bathymetry on their portals (SA's open data is Commonwealth HIPP and Kangaroo Island 1 m); AusENC/AHO: nothing open (archived hydro.gov.au pages show licence-only, $165 establishment fee and royalties, S-63 encrypted packs; the only open AHO data are the HIPP and reference surfaces on AusSeabed).
- External territories: Norfolk Island has a 1 m multibeam survey (July 2021, EM2040, CC BY 4.0); the brief's "lidar bathymetry" for Norfolk and Cocos is contradicted (no airborne lidar bathymetry for any external territory in GA eCat, data.gov.au or the WFS list). Cocos and Christmas have 2 m satellite-derived bathymetry (2022, 0-25 m) and 2010 GA grids; Lord Howe, Macquarie and Heard/McDonald GA grids (rows 140-150).
- Inland Australia: the only open river bathymetry found is CSIRO's Murray-Darling Basin bathymetry-embedded DEM v2 (5 m, 328.8 GB, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0). No reservoir survey of WaterNSW, Melbourne Water, Seqwater, SA Water, Water Corporation or Hydro Tasmania, and nothing for Lake Argyle, Menindee, the Lower Lakes or the ACT's lakes, was found open (row 177).
- New Zealand is not "GEBCO only" (contradicts the earlier survey): LINZ's Data Service lists 64 gridded multibeam and bathymetric-lidar "Depth Model" layers (1 m to 50 m, surveyed 1999-2025), each CC BY 4.0 and flagged downloadable (rows 209, 250-262; datum approximate LAT; whether the downloads need a free LINZ login was not tested), plus the nz-coastal S3 bucket's 1 m topobathy lidar (seabed to about 25 m, CC BY 4.0, NZVD2016; 2 m and 10 m versions exist per survey area but no national composite; row 205), plus LINZ sounding points, contours and chart images (CC BY) and footprint indexes of 906 bathymetric survey polygons. The lidar (NZVD2016) and multibeam (approximate LAT) do not share a datum.
- NIWA's 250 m NZ grid licence is resolved: NIWA's ArcGIS item text says CC BY-NC 4.0, the historical NIWA Open Data Licence is BY-NN-NC-SA, the publisher page says "all rights reserved": fine for a personal map, not commercial; only the 2008 CANZ zips were confirmed downloadable and the 2016 service is password-protected since 07/2025 per a mirror item (rows 200-203). DOC publishes a 10 m multibeam surface model for the NZ seabed (50 survey sets, about 52,000 km2) as an anonymous ArcGIS image service.
- NZ regional councils almost never publish bathymetry grids themselves: Marlborough (1 m contours of four multibeam surveys, CC BY 4.0), Hawke's Bay (coverage footprint only, grids by email), Wellington (re-serves LINZ contours) and Bay of Plenty (four anonymous ArcGIS contour layers: 12 Rotorua lakes, offshore, Tauranga Harbour from LINZ chart data, Maketu; licence and datum not stated, rows 400-403) have anything; Environment Canterbury has an unlicensed 2 m Banks Peninsula service. Auckland, Northland, Waikato, Bay of Plenty, Taranaki, Horizons, Nelson, Tasman, West Coast, Otago, Southland: nothing found. NZ lakes: no open digital grid found (NIWA's ~130 lake charts of 1966-89 are paper only, NZ$15 each; GNS has free PDF posters for seven Rotorua lakes; BoPRC has the lake contours above; LINZ raster charts for Taupo and Wakatipu). LINZ Data Service grid downloads need a free Koordinates ID: browsing needs none, the export API answers 401 without credentials, and the export flow was not tested. The realm: LINZ depth models for the Auckland Islands (5 m), Bounty/Antipodes (30 m), Snares and Solander (5 m lidar, 1999); Chatham, Kermadec, Campbell, Cook Islands, Niue and Tokelau have charts only.
- The Pacific islands: the only national-grade open grids are France's: Shom under Licence Ouverte 2.0, 100 m for New Caledonia (589 MB, to Vanuatu's southern islands; a model that includes GEBCO 2020), 100 m and 20 m for Tahiti/Moorea and for Clipperton (rows 300-305), plus the Ifremer Coral Sea Park multibeam synthesis at 10/25/50/100 m (CC BY 4.0; no download link found). Wallis and Futuna, the Tuamotu, Marquesas, Australs and Gambier: no Shom grid. PNG, Solomons, Vanuatu, Fiji, Tuvalu, Kiribati, Nauru, Samoa, Tonga, Pitcairn, Palau, FSM and the Marshalls (apart from Majuro) have no open national bathymetric grid; the lidar for Tuvalu, Tonga, Samoa, Vanuatu and Kiribati is request-only. The SPC Pacific Data Hub and pacific-data.sprep.org answer a Cloudflare challenge (not bypassed).
- Second-pass Pacific results: the Ifremer Coral Sea synthesis is not on SEANOE (Sextant DOIs 10.12770/..., no file link found; request from Sextant/SGNC); PANGAEA 770855 (Manus 35 m) is login-only with no licence stated; ISA DeepData's public map holds no bathymetry; new open Cook Islands multibeam (Nautilus NA176 EM302, CC BY 4.0, MGDS, row 500) and SONNE raw multibeam on PANGAEA for Tonga/Fiji (SO267), Kiribati (SO298), PNG (SO299, SO94), mostly CC BY 4.0 (SO94 CC BY 3.0; SO264 and SO299/2 restricted, rows 501-505); national GeoNodes and the Tuvalu/Tonga/Samoa/Vanuatu lidar stay request-only or unreachable.
- Best open atoll and island products: USGS Majuro 1 m topobathy DEM (2.36 GB, local mean sea level, public), SPC/GEM Majuro 5 m lagoon and 50 m reef multibeam via PacIOOS ("may be used and redistributed for free"), Easter Island CC0 30 m coastal grid, SEANOE Reao (CC BY) and Gambier (CC BY-ND: no derivative redistribution), a CSIRO satellite-derived Funafuti grid with no licence stated, and Zenodo/MGDS/PANGAEA research-cruise grids (Woodlark/Solomons, Ritter Island 25 m, Hunga Tonga 50 m, Tonga Trench and Lau, USGS Samoa Basin 50 m CC0, GA/CSIRO Coral Sea, Lord Howe Rise, Kermadec). Licences range from CC0 to CC BY-NC-SA 3.0 US; the CSIRO Kermadec set is CC BY-NC.
- GEBCO_2024 TID was sampled at 31 Pacific points (row 350): multibeam off New Caledonia, Efate, Suva, Samoa and Hunga; predicted at most lagoons, Tarawa, Vava'u and the Manus Basin (where a 35 m SO216 grid exists, so it is not in GEBCO_2024 there). NCEI multibeam footprint counts for 20 Pacific boxes are in row 358. ISA DeepData / Clarion-Clipperton Zone: no bathymetry layer or licence found.
- Open for a personal offline cache (licence read): everything CC BY 4.0 above (GA/AusSeabed, IMAS, WA DoT, NT, LINZ, Shom Licence Ouverte 2.0), CC0 sets, USGS public domain. Non-commercial or share-alike (fine for a personal cache): NIWA grids (CC BY-NC 4.0), CSIRO MDB DEM (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0), CSIRO Kermadec, most MGDS sets. Not cleared: the 1,247 AusSeabed "Restricted" surveys, AHO full-resolution surfaces (on request), NSW and Victoria files behind a WAF or login, AHO Reference Surfaces Adelaide (empty licence field), Gambier (ND).
- Legal limits on depth data: none found beyond "not for navigation" notices in Australia, NZ and the Pacific pages read. Licence discrepancies to note: Darwin metadata zip says CC BY 3.0 where GA's record says 4.0; NSW single-beam on AODN CC BY 4.0 (the earlier survey said CC BY 3.0 AU); Queensland Gold Coast CC BY 2.5 AU in CKAN but 4.0 in the AusSeabed register.
- Earlier survey (RESEARCH-BATHYMETRY.md): see section 7. Confirmed: the IMAS composite (10 m to 210 m, CC BY 4.0, footprint WFS), the GA 250 m 2023 grid being superseded, Shom 100 m Licence Ouverte 2.0. Contradicted: NSW "CC BY 3.0 AU"; "GA 2023 record names no open licence" holds only for 2023 (2024 and 2026 say CC BY 4.0); "NIWA 250 m licence unverified / NZ treated as unverified" (NZ has LINZ open grids); "Pacific island states not searched". Not confirmed: the IMAS "COG" format claim.

Row numbers run through all tables and skip (blocks: A 1-99, B 100-199, C 200-299, D 300-399). Rows that are pointers are marked Kind = pointer.

### 2.7 Regional councils and harbours

LINZ's depth models (grouped rows 250-262) and the nz-coastal DEM (row 205) cover almost every region; the council rows say what each council itself publishes. Portal searches used each council's ArcGIS Hub search API (`/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=`) with q = bathymetry, multibeam, depth, harbour, seabed, lake, sounding, sonar, hydrographic, lakebed, estuary.

Bathymetry for the council exists as NIWA-held data (rows 203, 204) and LINZ models (row 251) | | | | | NIWA hosts "Hauraki Bathymetry" (ARC23303) and "Manukau Harbour Bathymetry" web-map services for Auckland Council (item 562feb7e4e49483bb1c153681241c9e6, licence text "Please check with data owner if you want to use this dataset", access "NIWA 2023"; the service answered with no layer list, so not verified as a data source; the Manukau tile item is row 204). | 358 | **NCEI multibeam survey footprints (counts for this area)** | NOAA NCEI | pointer | - | Count of surveys intersecting envelopes (layer `Multibeam Bathymetric Surveys`, maxRecordCount 2000): PNG 45; Manus and New Britain 14; Solomons 17; Vanuatu 11; New Caledonia 11; Fiji 39 (to 180 E); Tonga 59; Samoa and American Samoa 63; Tuvalu and western Kiribati 12; French Polynesia 69; Wallis and Futuna 5; Marshalls 23; FSM 45; Palau 21; Nauru 0; Pitcairn 2; Easter 22; Hawaii 505; Guam and CNMI 114; Cook Islands 22 (agent C's area); eastern Kiribati 69 | - | - | - | ArcGIS REST query (`returnCountOnly=true`) | NCEI raw multibeam: no restrictions per the global report | https://gis.ngdc.noaa.gov/arcgis/rest/services/web_mercator/multibeam_dynamic/MapServer/0 |
| 359 | **Pacific Data Hub / SPC GEM / SPREP: nothing readable** | SPC, SPREP | pointer | - | pacificdata.org, the dataset search and the GeoNode/GeoServer hosts return HTTP 403 with `cf-mitigated: challenge` (Cloudflare); not bypassed. https://pacific-data.sprep.org/ answers 200 but `/resource?query=bathymetry` is 403 and `/dataset?query=...` and `/api/3/action/package_list` are 404. https://gsd.spc.int/ is a legacy Joomla site (latest news 2018; its repository page https://gsd.spc.int/maps-and-spatial-data-repository lists logos only; geonetwork.spc.int and pcrafi.sopac.org do not resolve); https://gem.spc.int/ (200) has a "Data & Tools" menu that was not opened. Wayback Machine: "Temporarily Offline" | - | - | - | The one SPC dataset reached was via PacIOOS (row 336) and the USGS Majuro metadata (row 335) | - | - |
| 360 | **JAMSTEC and Ifremer Pacific sets (SEANOE)** | JAMSTEC, Ifremer | pointer | - | Ifremer sets found: rows 301, 309, 310, 311. JAMSTEC: not searched (no JAMSTEC data host fetched) | - | - | - | - | - | - |


## 3. Notes per jurisdiction

### A: Australia Commonwealth and eastern states

### Commonwealth: GA, AusSeabed, AHO
- GA eCat record for the 2026 national grid: https://ecat.ga.gov.au/geonetwork/srv/api/search/records/_search (id 7ff1f558-8ef4-427b-9696-007b76aab2c6). Quotes: "In areas where no sounding data are available, the bathymetry is provided by an amended surface derived from 2-arc minute ETOPO ... and 1-arc minute ETOPO"; "Where overlapping data from multiple sensor types exists, the highest quality source was retained. TID codes follow GEBCO standards". Heard Island/McDonald Islands and the Antarctic Territory are excluded; use row 11 for HIMI. The 2024 and 2023 grids are the predecessors; the 2009 grid is the original (its record states CC BY 4.0).
- Footprint services: the eCat records for the "AusSeabed Bathymetry Survey Acquisitions Coverage Database" and "Compilations Coverage Database" link https://warehouse.ausseabed.gov.au/geoserver/wms and /wfs. The WFS type names are MARINEDATAREGISTER_ACQUISITIONS_INDEX and MARINEDATAREGISTER_COMPILATIONS_INDEX (the layer name in the eCat link, AusSeabed_Bathymetry_Surveys_Coverage, is stale: GetFeature answers "Could not find type"). The WFS also lists about 500 per-survey raster layers (Bathymetry, BathymetryHillshade, BathymetryShallow, Coverage), including AHO reference surfaces, so tiles can be fetched as WMS.
- Register counts (fetched 2026-11-13): 3,295 acquisitions, of which 243 PUBLISHED and 3,034 REGISTERED (known, held elsewhere); LEGAL_CONSTRAINTS: CC BY 4.0 2,042, Restricted 1,247, CC BY-NC 4.0 3, French Open Licence 2.0 1, empty 2. Largest custodians: WA Department of Transport 1,661 (916 Restricted), NSW Survey Authority 431 (all CC BY 4.0), Geoscience Australia 388, CSIRO 211, Fremantle Port Authority 167 (164 Restricted), AAD 150, City of Gold Coast 88, JAMSTEC 40, Deakin 35. "Restricted" or EMBARGO rows are not offline-usable. The register includes rows with DATA_URL "N/A" even when LEGAL_CONSTRAINTS says CC BY 4.0 (Deakin), so a licence cell there is not a download.
- files.ausseabed.gov.au is an S3 bucket (HEAD: server AmazonS3, 200). GA's older cloudfront host d28rz98at9flks.cloudfront.net still serves earlier zips. GA's services.ga.gov.au REST/WMS answers HTTP 403 (CloudFront "Request blocked") to this client for the root and the tried service paths; not worked around.
- AHO: HIPP surveys and Reference Surfaces are published by GA under CC BY 4.0 with "(c) Commonwealth of Australia (Australian Hydrographic Office)". The AHO supplies ENC tile spot depths as the broad-scale input to the 250 m grid; no open ENC dataset was found (row 26).
- NIDEM and DEA Intertidal: DEA Intertidal page https://knowledge.dea.ga.gov.au/data/product/dea-intertidal/: "Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International Licence"; elevation is relative to "modelled Mean Sea Level"; 10 m, 2016-2024, yearly. NIDEM 25 m (eCat a31425c7) is deprecated and superseded by eCat 149403.

###### Great Barrier Reef
- gbr30 (record 0f4e635c...): four grids A-D overlapping, WGS 84, approximate MSL; "Not to be used for navigation". gbr100 (record 5c47b141...): CRS GDA94 listed. deepreef.org page https://www.deepreef.org/2010/07/06/3dgbr-bathy/ names the dataset "gbr100", ~3,000,000 km2, notes the equivalent AusSeabed layer "Great Barrier Reef Bathymetry 2020 - 100m", and lists V6 10 November 2020 downloads.
- Satellite shallow bathymetry: GBR10 SDB (GBRMPA / EOMAP, ArcGIS item a7dcf3435d2f4c29816e62c873945f93); the register's compilation row names the owner "Earth Observation and Environmental Services (EOMAP)" and source "Industry" yet marks no embargo; GBRMPA's license text is "Creative Commons - Attribution 4.0 International license". Gold Coast and Gladstone SDB (EOMAP) in the register are embargoed.
- Falkor surveys: Southern GBR shelf 2-64 m (2 GB zip) and Northern GBR (4-32 m and 64 m zips) are CC BY 4.0 per the records.

###### New South Wales
- data.nsw.gov.au CKAN is open (https://data.nsw.gov.au/data/api/3/action/package_search): 25 hits for "bathymetry". The SEED API host datasets.seed.nsw.gov.au answers HTTP 202 with no body and the download hosts datasets.seed.nsw.gov.au and www.planningportal.nsw.gov.au answer 202 with header x-amzn-waf-action: challenge (JavaScript challenge): not bypassed. The REST services at mapprod2/mapprod3.environment.nsw.gov.au answer normally.
- Licence: all NSW DCCEEW records above carry license_id "cc-by" and license_url http://www.opendefinition.org/licenses/cc-by (version not stated). The AODN single-beam record states "Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License". The earlier survey's "CC BY 3.0 AU" is not what these records say.
- Datum: lidar and multibeam grids are relative to Australian Height Datum (AHD), not MSL/LAT; convert if merging with GA MSL grids.
- Who holds what: statewide mosaic (SEED/Planning Portal), 304 single-beam surveys on AODN, multibeam grids on AODN, marine lidar point clouds on ELVIS. The NSW record for lidar says "Raw and classified LAS format files ... to be made available on the ELVIS website".

###### Victoria
- discover.data.vic.gov.au CKAN is open (8 hits for "bathymetry"). VCDEM 2021 notes: "Contains gridded data sources with a gridded spatial resolution (pixel size) equal or superior to 10m in addition to vertical uncertainty meeting IHO Order 1b specifications (ie better than 0.5m)". DEECA Datashare is a login/cart order system: https://datashare.maps.vic.gov.au; the Vicmap Elevation ArcGIS services (METRO 1-5 m, STATEWIDE 10-20 m) are contour/relief feature services listed on the Vicmap Elevation REST API record (CC BY 4.0), not bathymetric rasters.
- Port Phillip contours are chart-digitised (1949-1985 surveys, three datum families); useful for display, not measurement. Gippsland Lakes, Western Port, Corner Inlet, Mallacoota: same VFA licence, same product type.

###### Queensland
- www.data.qld.gov.au CKAN: only the Sunshine Coast bathymetric lidar and the MSQ series are bathymetric. The QSpatial catalogue (qldspatial.information.qld.gov.au) is a search page; the XML document endpoint answers and gives the Sunshine Coast lidar's CRS and lineage. Qld Globe and the state lidar (ELVIS, 3-year delay) are topography, not bathymetry (see terrain-rest.md 2.3).
- Gold Coast: City of Gold Coast publishes its DTM on data.gov.au (4.1 GB, CC BY 2.5 AU in CKAN) and its waterway surveys feed the AusSeabed register (CC BY 4.0).

###### Tasmania
- The LIST (services.thelist.tas.gov.au) MarineAndCoastal MapServer holds the bathymetry contours; the OpenData WFS (2,000 features per request) holds lidar indexes with the CC BY 3.0 AU statement. IMAS SeaMap contours are CC BY 4.0 and single-beam CC BY 3.0 AU. Storm Bay 5 m and the AusBathyTopo Bass Strait grid cover the sea off the Derwent and the north.

###### ACT
- No lake-bed bathymetry found in any open catalogue tried (row 62). ACT 2020 lidar (CC BY 4.0, from the terrain report) is topography.

###### Hawaii
- Not in this area's results; the North America report covers it.

### B: WA, SA, NT, external territories, inland

Western Australia
- DoT layers: Point Survey Index http://catalogue.data.wa.gov.au (via https://catalogue.data.wa.gov.au/api/3/action/package_search?q=bathymetry&rows=40, 18 results, only DoT and WAMSI relevant). Composite surfaces extent layer https://public-services.slip.wa.gov.au/public/rest/services/SLIP_Public_Services/Imagery_and_Maps/MapServer/19 ; its `url` values point at the S3 tiles named `<Place>_5m.tif`; the Broome tile (1,014,270 bytes) is float32 731 x 1350 with nodata -9999, depths negative, 19% of cells valid. The older name of the same layer is "Composite Surfaces Index"; the data.wa.gov.au entry says "This dataset was formerly known as - Composite Surfaces Index". The AODN-harvested copy is "DOT Bathymetry Composite Surfaces Index - Raw data" on data.gov.au.
- DoT point zips' readme: coordinates GDA94 MGA, Z in metres relative to AHD, "DoT does not provide any warranty as to the accuracy"; file suffixes _Single, _Levels, _DigiSingle, _Hand; "The majority of the surveys were originally captured using a local sounding datum, but they have all been converted to AHD datum. However there are issues inherent in this conversion." Contact for datum questions is named in the zip.
- The 2009 Two Rocks to Cape Naturaliste lidar metadata PDF is attached to the composite surfaces dataset; extents and accuracy as in row 104. No separate download for the lidar grid alone.
- Landgate: the Landgate DEM listing is a land DEM under "Personal Use License" (custom_other); excluded from bathymetry; no WA Landgate bathymetry product found. SLIP "WA_Bathymetry" MapServer holds 5 m image layers named `DOT_<area>_5m_Image_20151704.img` (https://public-services.slip.wa.gov.au/public/rest/services/Landgate_Public_Maps/WA_Bathymetry/MapServer).
- IMAS's national composite (Flukes 2026, https://metadata.imas.utas.edu.au/geonetwork/srv/api/records/69e9ac91-babe-47ed-8c37-0ef08f29338a) states it used "WA Bathymetry Portal" data to build its 10 m shallow zones for the whole EEZ, including WA; its licence link is CC BY 4.0 (creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/ in the record). That is a national row for agent A; for WA it re-packages rows 100, 101 and 105.
- AIMS/WAMSI: the Ningaloo and Kimberley sets are small (rows 112 to 114). The AODN GeoNetwork JSON route (https://catalogue.aodn.org.au/geonetwork/srv/eng/q?...) answered; the Elasticsearch POST route returned HTTP 403 and was not pursued.

South Australia
- data.sa.gov.au has no bathymetry (CKAN "bathymetry" count 0). The DEW "South Australian Marine Benthic Habitats" set (cc-by) is habitat polygons for the Adelaide Dolphin Sanctuary, Upper Spencer Gulf, Upper Gulf St Vincent, Kangaroo Island and others, not depth. The Reservoir Volumes table (SA Water, cc-by) is volumes, not geometry.
- SA's coastal lidar (row 123) is catalogued as terrain; no bathymetric flag. The ELVIS catalogue zip includes CC_BY.txt.
- Commonwealth products give SA its depth data: rows 120 to 122, plus national rows 141 (250 m) and A's IMAS 10 m composite.

Northern Territory
- The Darwin programme is documented at the NT record https://data.nt.gov.au (package `darwin-harbour-habitat-mapping-program`) and in GA eCat. Programme figures quoted by NT: "In total, this data package extends over an area of 1978 km2, including 1754 km2 mapped using multibeam echosounders, during four marine surveys over 247 days."
- The downloaded Metadata.txt (inside the 127094_03_0.zip) gives the grid resolution (1 m), MSL, WGS84 UTM 52S, 10 cm RMS positional accuracy from C-NAV and Applanix attitude accuracy values; it says "The accuracy is high but it is cell size dependent. Note that the water depth dictates the cell size".
- GA's data.nt.gov.au copy of the package is a 4.39 MB zip (HEAD), so the 3.5 GB grid must come from GA.

External territories
- The brief's "2021 lidar bathymetry" for Norfolk and "2020s lidar bathymetry" for Cocos did not match any record: Norfolk 2021 is EM2040 Mark II multibeam (row 140); Cocos 2022 is satellite-derived (row 146). GA's records all state "not to be used for navigational purposes".
- Norfolk: "Full resolution surfaces available from AHO on request under restricted licence" is carried in the record text (copied HIPP wording), although the 1 m GeoTIFF is the open file; take the record as stating both.
- Christmas and Cocos DEMs 2011 on data.gov.au (Department of Infrastructure) are terrain; the Cocos Island DEM 2011 listing shows licence cc-nc (not fetched further; terrain scope).
- HIMI is excluded from GA's 250 m national grid by its own text; use rows 149 and 150.
- Australian Antarctic stations: only a pointer (row 151).

Inland
- CSIRO's MDB DEM v2 is the only open river bathymetry set found; it is a flood-modelling input, not survey-grade for every reach. Lake Eyre's GSSA survey is topography of a dry bed (42 m cells).
- Queensland (Seqwater) and the other state water corporations hold reservoir surveys that are not in any open portal queried (row 177).

### C: New Zealand and its realm

New Zealand national
- Licensing page: "You can use, remix, distribute, and build on Toitu Te Whenua data, even commercially, as much as you like via our open Creative Commons license"; "Almost all data from the LINZ Data Service is provided under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International licence" (https://www.linz.govt.nz/products-services/data/licensing-and-using-data). Aerial imagery and personal-information layers are exceptions.
- LINZ hydrographic page (https://www.linz.govt.nz/products-services/data/types-linz-data/hydrographic-data): "The majority of our bathymetric data is available as georeferenced TIFF images of the original hard copy sounding sheets. Data collected after 2000 is often available as ... dense point clouds, thinned point clouds, or a 3-dimensional surface model"; "The LDS does not hold bathymetric data, but instead provides indexes ... All requests for data must be made by emailing hydro@linz.govt.nz". This page is out of date against the LDS API, which lists 64 downloadable grid layers (CC BY 4.0) published from 2024-03 (e.g. https://data.linz.govt.nz/services/api/v1.x/layers/116014/ : kind grid, `public_access: download`, licence 171 CC BY 4.0, "Data comprises a gridded depth model as tif or ascii tiles in UTM projection. Vertical accuracy is +/- 0.3m (95%) ... Vertical datum is Approximate Lowest Astronomical Tide").
- LDS API: the Koordinates API `https://data.linz.govt.nz/services/api/v1.x/layers/?q=...` and `/layers/<id>/` answer anonymously (layer lists, descriptions, licences, extents); WFS/WMTS/raster-query services need an API key (`auth_method: apikey`). Whether exports of these grids need a login was not tested (I did not log in).
- Category listing used for the 64 layers: https://data.linz.govt.nz/services/api/v1.x/layers/?page_size=100&category=hydrographic-maritime/bathymetric-surfaces (64 results).
- 3DCM programme: pages https://www.linz.govt.nz/products-services/data/3d-coastal-mapping and sub-pages (access-data, mapping-technologies, questions-and-answers). Scope "map up to 40% of New Zealand's coastline extending, nominally, 200m inland of the Mean High-Water Spring (MHWS) coastline and seaward to a water depth of 25m ... just over 11,250 square kilometres"; "Bathymetric LiDAR needs clear water ... In areas of turbidity ... we will use ... multi-beam echo sounder"; lidar in NZVD2016 and "The biggest challenge arises when we want to join our existing bathymetry data, that may be in ... Mean Sea Level or Lowest Astronomical Tide" (a 'Joining Land and Sea' tool is in scope). Practical consequence: nz-coastal (NZVD2016) and the LDS depth models (approx. LAT) are on different vertical datums.
- nz-coastal bucket: STAC `catalog.json` fetched (https://nz-coastal.s3.ap-southeast-2.amazonaws.com/catalog.json): 28 survey collections at 1 m DEM, 26 at 2 m, 26 at 10 m, 26 at 1 m DSM (counted from the child links), plus hillshade collections and a New Zealand composite (65 items, 2024-04-11 to 2026-05-03). AWS registry https://registry.opendata.aws/nz-coastal/ : "seabed down to approximately 25m in depth ... nationally consistent 1m resolution tiles"; licence "CC-BY-4.0". The earlier survey's "seabed to about 25 m, CC BY 4.0" is confirmed; the 2 m and 10 m versions exist as per-survey collections. nz-elevation (224 collections, catalog fetched) holds no seabed collections (its only coastal-titled items are Otago Coastal Catchments land DEM).
- NIWA 250 m licence: sources reconciled above (rows 200-201). The NIWA download page was only reachable as a Wayback copy (https://web.archive.org/web/2024/https://niwa.co.nz/environmental-information/download-bathymetry-data resolves to the 2025-01-18 capture); the live URL returns 403 and was not worked round. NIWA Hub items (BoP 25 m, NZOI swaths, Tuaheni Landslide 25 m) all carry CC BY-NC 4.0 (API https://data-niwa.opendata.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=bathymetry), and the Hauraki and Manukau items carry no open licence.
- data.govt.nz: the CKAN API route answered the "Pardon Our Interruption" bot page; not worked round.
- GNS Science: "Bathymetry of Te Riu-a-Maui / Zealandia" StoryMap (2020 poster) is a poster/interactive map; its description says it is available as an interactive map, pdf and poster; no grid download seen (https://storymaps.arcgis.com/stories/a34446d9c3ea4e8bab16547c002476c2).

Regions (rows 230-262)
- All council claims come from the Hub APIs named in the rows; "0" means the search returned zero items for that term, not that the council has nothing offline.
- Marlborough: data are 1 m contours (not grids), datum text differs by survey (LAT (NIWA) vs NZVD2009); CC BY 4.0.
- HBRC: only the coverage polygons are open; "Copyright of these data are are jointly vested with HBRC and NIWA".
- LINZ coverage gaps in the depth-model series: no layers for Manukau Harbour, Kaipara, Hauraki beyond the east coast layers, Wellington south coast beyond Porirua and the entrance, Cook Strait, the West Coast (South Island), Kapiti, Wairarapa, Canterbury north of Banks Peninsula except Kaikoura, Timaru, Foveaux east, Chatham Islands. 3DCM polygons show unfinished coastal lidar work for West Coast (Greymouth, Westport, Hokitika), Canterbury (Timaru, Kaikoura, Lyttelton), Kapiti/Foxton, Manukau, Hawke's Bay Napier and Wairoa-Mahia, Marlborough Cloudy Bay.

Realm
- Everything for the realm is on the LDS (CC BY 4.0), as raster charts and the sub-Antarctic models; indexes show LINZ holds more (SW Pacific 161 surface models, 201 data polygons) that must be requested by email.

### D: Pacific islands and compilations

- New Caledonia. Shom façade grid and the Government's own mirror (rows 300-301): the Shom grid's genealogy (data.gouv.nc text) names GEBCO Grid 2020 and filled SRTM among its inputs, so it is a model, not pure measurements; both PBMA and NM packages exist (PBMA 588.9 MB). Ifremer's park synthesis is multibeam only and cut at the park boundary; the lagoon is out of it. https://www.data.gouv.fr/api/1/datasets/658f61cfa7440c6c76c71b4b/ ; https://data.gouv.nc/api/explore/v2.1/catalog/datasets?where=search(%22bathym%22)&limit=10 ; https://api.datacite.org/dois/10.12770/3db7cb0d-a13d-437f-af2f-c19047f02f83 . georep.nc itself is a portal of map viewers (home page https://georep.nc/ lists "Mer et littoral, Bathymetrie du parc naturel de la ..."); no Litto3D New Caledonia dataset exists in Shom's list or data.gouv.nc results (the terrain report says one is announced).
- French Polynesia. Shom grids for Tahiti and Moorea only, 100 m façade and 20 m lagoons (rows 302-304), requested by Meteo-France and "10 km band" limited; lidar 2015 is WMS only (row 306); Shom's own lists have no façade grid for the Tuamotu, Marquesas, Australs or Gambier. SEANOE lagoon grids for Reao and Gambier (rows 309-310). Te Fenua / data.gouv.fr: only the Shom organisation list (122 datasets) was read; no Polynesian government bathymetry dataset was seen in the data.gouv.fr search for "shom mnt bathymetrie" (3 hits, all Mediterranean/Ifremer isobaths).
- Wallis and Futuna: nothing (row 308). Shom surveys exist (19 features).
- Clipperton (France, East Pacific): two Shom grids (row 305); the 20 m URL on data.gouv.fr is mangled (`file.` for `file/`).
- Marshall Islands: Majuro only (rows 335-337). The USGS TBDEM includes SPC/SOPAC multibeam (SOPAC 2008, Kruger and Kumar) and SDB; SPC/GEM's 5 m lagoon and 50 m reef grids are on PacIOOS with a redistributable licence text. No grid for Kwajalein or the outer atolls.
- American Samoa: see NAMERICA rows 99-101 (PIBHMC 5/20/40 m, NCEI 10/90 m) confirmed by the PacIOOS ERDDAP ids; add USGS Samoa Basin (EEZ, 50 m, CC0) and MGDS FK171224.
- Palau, FSM: no sea-floor product open; the terrain report (terrain-rest.md, section 3.3) names FSM 10 m land DEMs, confirmed in the PacIOOS ids.
- PNG, Solomon Islands, Vanuatu, Fiji, Samoa, Tonga, Tuvalu, Kiribati, Nauru, Pitcairn: only research-cruise grids (rows 313-318, 324, 328-330), NCEI footprints and GEBCO. National sites: https://www.nmsa.gov.pg/ HTTP 500; https://www.vmgd.gov.vu/ and https://www.mnre.gov.ws/ answered 200 (home pages only; no data pages read); the Solomon Islands fisheries host did not resolve. No national legal text limiting publication of depths was found for any of these.
- Easter Island: Zenodo CC0 interpolated coastal grid (row 333); NCEI 22 surveys; SAMERICA has no row (row 334).
- Hawaii, Guam, CNMI: pointers (rows 339-340).
### Second pass (rows 400-515 and the corrected rows)

**Second-pass notes, New Zealand**

- LDS downloads need a login (task 3). Evidence: (1) the LINZ guide page "Register for the LDS": "Register for a Koordinates ID to start downloading data from the LINZ Data Service" (https://www.linz.govt.nz/guidance/data-service/linz-data-service-guide/getting-started-lds/register-lds); (2) the Getting-started page says "You do not need to sign in or register to explore our data" (browse/search/view only), and describes the Export and Crop buttons and a raster "Tiles table ... download individual raster tiles in its native format" (https://www.linz.govt.nz/guidance/data-service/linz-data-service-guide/getting-started-lds); (3) the Koordinates API: layer records answer anonymously and say `public_access: "download"`, `user_permissions: ["find","view","download"]`, `user_capabilities` incl. `can-export` (https://data.linz.govt.nz/services/api/v1.x/layers/116615/), but `GET https://data.linz.govt.nz/services/api/v1.x/exports/` returns HTTP 401 "Authentication credentials were not provided", and the layer's `datasources` link returns 404 anonymously; (4) every LDS web service or API (WFS, WMTS, changesets) needs an API key, created under an account (https://www.linz.govt.nz/guidance/data-service/linz-data-service-guide/web-services : "To use any LINZ Data Service web service or API, you will first need to create an API key"). Conclusion: the free account is the only gate (no payment, no application, no per-layer approval seen); the grids are "download" for any registered user. I did not register or log in, so the actual export flow (format list, size limits, queue time) is untested. The data-set pages themselves are a JavaScript app (the HTML has only `<div id="root">`), so only the API shows layer details. A registration is a one-time human step; scripting the export afterwards via the Koordinates export API with a key is the expected route (not tested).
- LINZ also publishes an Esri-side org (`https://services.arcgis.com/xdsHIIxuCWByZiCB/arcgis/rest/services?f=json`, owner hydro_LINZ): rendered "tiff" map tile services of the survey grids (HS69_Stewart_tiff, HS72_Taranaki_tiff, HS70_Banks_tiff, HS73_Tasman_tiff, HS75_Napier_tiff; `https://tiles.arcgis.com/tiles/xdsHIIxuCWByZiCB/arcgis/rest/services/HS75_Napier_tiff/MapServer` etc.) and HYPLAN hydrographic plan feature services. These are tile pictures of the grids (no depth values) and need no key for viewing, but give no data.
- Marlborough Sounds "HS51" is a single layer on the LDS (116627), already in row 256; see row 407 for survey dates and accuracy. No other Marlborough Sounds multibeam grid beyond the three on row 256 (Pelorus 2019, QCS/Tory 2016, Port Underwood 2023/2025) was found by an LDS search for Marlborough, HS51, Pelorus, Queen Charlotte, Admiralty Bay, D'Urville, Kenepuru, Tory Channel (the Marlborough DC contours of row 239 cover Admiralty Bay/D'Urville).
- Lakes summary (task 1): no digital lake bathymetry grid from NIWA/Earth Sciences NZ, Environment Southland, Waikato RC, Otago RC or Meridian was found published. Open digital lake bathymetry in NZ found: BoPRC contours for 12 Rotorua-area lakes (row 400); GNS PDF poster maps for seven of them (row 404). Paper only: NIWA lake charts (row 405, 1966-1989). Surveyed but data not found published: Wanaka 2024, Whakatipu 2019 (row 406). LINZ's raster charts for Taupo (232) and Wakatipu (252) are on the LDS (rows 213, 270).
- Lake Tekapo "first time a lake floor had been mapped in New Zealand", 2 years before 2019 (NIWA Whakatipu news page) : the Tekapo data is also not on the DataHub list.
- LINZ P1030 Lake Wakatipu (LINZ hydrographic survey 2003, single and multibeam): seen only in a search-result snippet; its data.govt.nz catalogue page returned an Imperva "Pardon Our Interruption" bot challenge (not worked round). If real, this is the only known LINZ lake survey; it may be in the LDS "NZ Bathymetric Data Index" (row 211) but I did not query that index (WFS needs a key).
- NIWA ArcGIS portal text search for lake terms (Taupo, Rotorua, Wakatipu, Wanaka, Te Anau, Manapouri; "lake bathymetry New Zealand"): 0 lake bathymetry items; DataCite searches (Tekapo/Wakatipu/Wanaka/Taupo/Manapouri/Te Anau + bathymetry): 0 records; "Lake bathymetry New Zealand": only a Rotomahana terraces paper.

**Second-pass notes, Australia**

- AODN catalogue: the working endpoint is `https://catalogue.aodn.org.au/geonetwork/srv/eng/q?_content_type=json&fast=index&any=<terms>&resultType=details&to=15` (the `catalogue-imos.aodn.org.au` host returns the same IMOS-heavy index; both 200). Searching it for bathymetry names finds only the two NSW OEH records (multibeam 60160b01-8ffc-45ce-a6f4-ee70ce391ec6, single-beam 8b2ddb75-2f29-4552-af6c-eac9b02156a6), the Ningaloo WAMSI record and the IMOS AUV Sirius campaigns; nothing for NSW lidar, VCDEM, Sydney Harbour (only IMOS moorings), Moreton Bay (plankton/colour data), Gulf St Vincent (moorings, radar), Lake Macquarie (0).
- AODN S3 archive: the bucket explorer `https://data.aodn.org.au/` is a static page; direct object URLs of the form `https://data.aodn.org.au/NSW-OEH/Multi-beam/2005/<survey>/<file>` are anonymous. `?list-type=2` listing returns the explorer page, so use the WFS CSV for paths.
- NSW CKAN (data.nsw.gov.au): `package_search?q=bathymetry` gave 25 hits; the CKAN API sometimes answers 500 when `fl=` is used (plain queries work). Open-licence (cc-by) marine records beyond rows 40-45: Seabed NSW program record, Seabed Classification, NSW seabed reef extent, Wollongong and Shellharbour seabed landforms derived from MBES 2022, NSW seabed landforms derived from marine lidar 2022, Hunter Marine Park MBES surveys, HABMAP coverage areas, and WRL estuarine tidal hydrodynamic models for Tweed, Richmond, Clarence, Hastings, Macleay, Manning, Shoalhaven (RMA-2 finite-element meshes under Apache licence, which contain bed levels but are models, not surveys; https://data.nsw.gov.au/data/api/3/action/package_show?id=coastal-floodplain-prioritisation-study-estuarine-tidal-hydrodynamic-models). Download resources of these records sit on datasets.seed.nsw.gov.au or www.planningportal.nsw.gov.au, both CloudFront WAF-challenged; not bypassed.
- AusSeabed register by custodian (WFS, 3,295 rows, https://warehouse.ausseabed.gov.au/geoserver/wfs?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=ausseabed:MARINEDATAREGISTER_ACQUISITIONS_INDEX&outputFormat=application/json): NSW Survey Authority 431 rows, whose DATA_URL is `portal.aodn.org.au` for 304, `elevation.fsdf.org.au` for 124 and "N/A" for 3; by type Singlebeam 298, Multibeam 80, LiDAR 47, direct measurements 6. So the 431 NSW rows are all served from AODN or ELVIS, never from the SEED/Planning Portal. 80 NSW multibeam rows match the 67 AODN survey folders plus later surveys.
- Victoria: GA or Deakin or the DEECA custodians contribute only the rows 458, 459 and 51; no Vic government custodian appears in the register; so VCDEM is not in the register either.
- Queensland: QSpatial catalogue API is the Esri Geoportal REST `https://qldspatial.information.qld.gov.au/catalogue/rest/find/document?searchText=<terms>&f=json&max=20` (anonymous, 200). Searches: bathymetry (2), lidar bathymetry (465, mostly land lidar), satellite derived bathymetry (207, mostly imagery), hydrographic (1: hydrographic features series), seabed (0), multibeam (0), sounding (0), Gladstone (48, none bathymetric). data.qld.gov.au CKAN: bathymetry (1), hydrographic survey (1: "Hydrographic Charts 1802-2013" at Queensland State Archives, a 4 MB CSV/6.5 MB JSON catalogue of scanned chart items, CC BY 4.0, not depth data: https://www.data.qld.gov.au/api/3/action/package_show?id=hydrographic-charts-1802-2013), Great Barrier Reef bathymetry (66, none bathymetric apart from seismic track charts), "Seabed characteristic data" (SeaScan, Wide Bay, 31 Aug 2007, a 2.2 MB ASCII point file, CC BY 3.0).
- Tasmania: the harvest source for the data.gov.au stubs is `https://data.thelist.tas.gov.au/datagn/srv/eng/csw`; `GetRecords` with `AnyText like '%bathymetr%'` returned 17 records including "Tasmania 2 metre Digital Elevation Model" (land DEM: 65% from lidar, 2 m, GDA94 MGA 55, vertical AHD, 10 m outside lidar; link to ELVIS), "Bathymetric Polygons" and "Bathymetric Contours" (100 m intervals, ~150 km around Tasmania). Row 57's IMAS SeaMap contours are the same TAFI survey family as the 5 m contour records of row 460.
- South Australia: DataCite finds no Lower Lakes depth grid. Related items: CSIRO "Stitching elevation and bathymetry data for the Murray River and Lower Lakes, South Australia" (Austin and Gallant, 2010, a publication record: https://api.datacite.org/dois/10.25919/bthh-b684 , landing page publications.csiro.au answered 403 to HEAD); University of Adelaide "Reconnecting the Waters ... barrage transparency" report 2026 (CC BY 4.0, a hydrodynamic study of the Coorong, Lower Lakes and Murray Mouth, https://api.datacite.org/dois/10.25909/33337062); Tibby et al. Lower Lakes diatom data and hydrodynamic model outputs (CC BY 4.0, salinity series, no bed model: https://api.datacite.org/dois/10.25909/19642329.v2). Row 170's CSIRO bathymetry-embedded MDB DEM remains the only grid-type lead for the Murray; whether it covers Lake Alexandrina/Albert was not read.
- Reservoirs and lakes, one route each plus second route: Lake Argyle: data.gov.au "Lake Argyle" (399 hits, all gazettes/other), "Ord River" (83, none depth), catalogue.data.wa.gov.au "Lake Argyle" (4: Landgate DEMs listing, WAMSI), DataCite (0 for bathymetry/DEM), Zenodo (2 hits, a modelling-tool paper). Menindee: data.nsw.gov.au (2: WaterNSW "DSEP Menindee Lakes Storages V3.0", Dam Safety Emergency Plan, licence lsba, resource on flooddata.ses.nsw.gov.au), data.gov.au (354; GA AEM and gravity sets), DataCite (an arXiv 2026 preprint on airborne electromagnetic surveys of Lake Menindee under varying water levels, CC BY 4.0, a conductivity study not a depth grid: https://api.datacite.org/dois/10.48550/arxiv.2603.19933 ). Lower Lakes: rows 126, 461 and the South Australia note. Lake Eildon: discover.data.vic.gov.au "Eildon" (2: town profiles), "reservoir bathymetry" (0), "storage capacity survey" (4: Melbourne Water storage dam polygons, no depths), DataCite "Lake Eildon" (nothing relevant), Zenodo (0). Warragamba: data.nsw.gov.au "Warragamba" (47: flood studies, plus the 2002 REF PDF record fis_00169), "Lake Burragorang" (21: Nattai flood-study files), "dam bathymetry" (0), Zenodo and DataCite (0). Lake Burley Griffin: data.act.gov.au Socrata catalogue `https://www.data.act.gov.au/api/catalog/v1?q=bathymetry` (39 hits, all federated from other jurisdictions or the US; the only Lake Burley Griffin hit is a flood layer), data.gov.au (64, gazettes and one ACT flood layer), DataCite (two 1967-69 UNSW sediment reports, CC BY-NC-ND 3.0 and not depth grids: https://api.datacite.org/dois/10.4225/53/579819db839f1).
- AHO (via Wayback, since www.hydro.gov.au is current but was reported unreachable earlier): archived pages fetched 2026-11-13 from web.archive.org snapshots of 2026-09-08/09. Licensing page (https://web.archive.org/web/20260908131750id_/https://www.hydro.gov.au/hydrography/licensing): "To access, reproduce or publish AHO material, you must obtain written permission from the Commonwealth through a licence agreement with the AHO"; fees and royalties may apply; AHO can refuse. Copyright page (https://web.archive.org/web/20260908131745id_/https://www.hydro.gov.au/copyright): all AHO material is under the Copyright Act 1968; written licence needed to reproduce, modify, derive, supply to anyone else, or use commercially; "Only in exceptional circumstances will we grant a licence to make reproductions of our material". Hydrographic Data page (https://web.archive.org/web/20260909150848id_/https://www.hydro.gov.au/hydrography/hydrographic-data): AHO holds bathymetry, wrecks, tides; accessible only "under licence"; the AHO "publishes some data through the AusSeabed Project", i.e. HIPP surfaces and reference surfaces (rows 18, 19). AusENC page (https://web.archive.org/web/20260909090633id_/https://www.hydro.gov.au/charts/ausenc): official S-63-encrypted ENC cells (AU, PG, SB, plus NZ cells by LINZ), weekly updates, sold as packs. Pricing page (https://web.archive.org/web/20260909150848id_/https://www.hydro.gov.au/charts/pricing): AusENC Australia pack 12 months $793.50 (3 months $317.40), Voyager 12 months $402.50, Coastal $80.50, Port $23.00, licence establishment fee $165.00 and royalties of up to 35% of net selling price on derived products. Conclusion: no open AusENC and no open-licence AHO data other than the AusSeabed-published HIPP surfaces; ENC data is encrypted. The AHO "S-102 Bathymetric Surface" page was not archived (Wayback 404); the S-100 rollout exists as a menu item only. The Wayback availability API works (`https://archive.org/wayback/available?url=hydro.gov.au`, snapshot 2026-10-26); the CDX search service answered 503 "Temporarily Offline".
- Licence conflicts to carry: MSQ soundings CC BY 4.0 (record) vs CC-BY-3.0 (CKAN); LIST layer 4/25 CC BY 3.0 AU, layer 24 none; Darwin etc. unchanged.

**Second-pass notes, Pacific**

- Pacific Data Hub / national SPREP portals: the national instances answer 200 on their front page (https://niue-data.sprep.org/ , https://cookislands-data.sprep.org/ , https://samoa-data.sprep.org/ , https://tonga-data.sprep.org/ , https://vanuatu-data.sprep.org/ , https://solomonislands-data.sprep.org/ ) but every search URL (`/search?s=bathymetry`, `/dataset?query=`) returns 403 (or 404) for all 12 country hosts; not bypassed. These are Drupal (Niue front page links /dataset/..., /search?f[0]=content_type:dataset.dataset) with a 6-item Niue front page (SOE report, marine managed areas, no bathymetry).
- geonode.gov.vu (named by a search result as Vanuatu's GeoNode) timed out at 15 s over HTTP and a second attempt; other guessed national GeoNode/geoportal host names (fiji, samoa, tonga, tuvalu, kiribati, solomon, png, cook, niue, palau, fsm under gov.* and pacificdata.org subdomains) do not resolve. No national GeoNode for the Pacific states was reached.
- World Bank documents API (https://search.worldbank.org/api/v3/wds?format=json&qterm=bathymetry&count_exact=<country>) returned 0 documents for bathymetry and for lidar for Fiji, Samoa, Tonga, Tuvalu, Kiribati, Solomon Islands, PNG, Cook Islands, Palau and FSM; Vanuatu bathymetry: 1 (an Environmental Assessment for the Infrastructure Reconstruction and Improvement Project, http://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/649091468121736186/pdf/SFG2033-REVISED-PUBLIC-discosed-7-25-18.pdf , listed, not read).
- Zenodo API (https://zenodo.org/api/records?q=...) per country with bathymetry/multibeam/lidar/DEM: no new bathymetry grid. Hits are the starter kits (row 510), Zenodo 3794155 (Vityaz and Vanuatu trench GMT geomorphology, known), 7456324 (Hunga DEMs, row 328), 10257561 (row 313), PNG forearc multichannel seismic (13854291, 2.3 GB, seismic not bathymetry), and 17254446 (a Guam-area point cloud, land).
- PANGAEA Elasticsearch (https://ws.pangaea.de/es/pangaea/panmd/_search, 200): hit counts Fiji 18, Samoa 0, Tonga 2, Vanuatu 1 (foraminifera table, not bathymetry), Tuvalu 0, Kiribati 2, Solomon Islands 0, PNG 10, Cook Islands 0, Niue 0, Palau 0, FSM(Micronesia) 3 (SO113 underway oceanography, not bathymetry). Tonga 904800 is socio-environmental layers (no depth).
- DataCite queries per country (dataset type, bathymetry OR multibeam OR lidar OR DEM): other hits are NCEI cruise records, UCSD Scripps cruise data (10.6075/..., cruise legs, not grids) and USGS landslide layers for FSM (CC0).
- UNDP Tuvalu project: www.undp.org/pacific pages answer 403 (Akamai "Access Denied", not bypassed); info.undp.org/docs/pdc/... project documents answer 502 (HEAD); tcap.tv and tcap.gov.tv do not resolve; GCF https://www.greenclimate.fund/node/13157 is 404 but https://www.greenclimate.fund/project/fp015 is 200.
- Cook Islands: seabed data custodian is Infrastructure Cook Islands (hydrography and geoscience), with access by request (row 513); an open EM302 set from the Nautilus (row 500) is the first open Cook Islands multibeam found.

## 4. Non-governmental and commercial sources

### A: Australia Commonwealth and eastern states

- Beaman, R.J. (JCU, deepreef.org): gbr100, gbr30, Northern Australia, Bass Strait, Williams Ridge grids; same data as GA rows 6-12 (the deepreef page offers more formats and Google Earth hillshade).
- IMAS (University of Tasmania): SeaMap Tasmania (2007), Storm Bay 5 m (2025), the EEZ composite (row 20), Seamap Australia benthic layers (not bathymetry).
- CSIRO MNF and DAP: voyage grids (row 24); one voyage archive is CC BY-NC-SA 4.0.
- Schmidt Ocean Institute RV Falkor: FK200930 and FK201122 grids (rows 15-16, republished by GA).
- EOMAP (satellite-derived bathymetry, GBR10 and embargoed Gold Coast/Gladstone), and Deakin University (Victoria, row 51).

### B: WA, SA, NT, external territories, inland

- CSIRO Data Access Portal (HIMI 200 m, MDB DEM): rows 150, 170.
- UWA (Shark Bay SDB; NW Shelf DEM, Lebrec et al. 2021 ESSD method): rows 106, 108. UWA/Curtin (Ningaloo hyperspectral 2006, kriged single beam 2006 to 2008): rows 112, 113.
- EOMAP (commercial satellite-derived bathymetry delivered as open GA/Parks Australia data, rows 145, 146).
- Zenodo: WA wetland hypsometry (row 173), GRDL global reservoirs (row 174). Other Zenodo queries ("bathymetry Darwin Harbour", "Lake Eyre bathymetry", "Spencer Gulf") returned only taxonomic figure records.
- DataCite query "GLOBathy" returned no GLOBathy record (HydroLAKES v1.0, CC0, appeared; mean-depth attribute only; global report territory).
- PANGAEA and SOI/R2R cruise grids were not queried for this area (no query run); noted under Unverified.

### C: New Zealand and its realm

- Zenodo research-cruise grids (rows 217-219): TAN1808 north 25 m (CC BY 4.0), Southern Hikurangi 25/50 m (CC BY-NC 4.0), TAN2412 50/100 m (CC BY 4.0). Others seen, not tabled: 14903185 (Chatham Rise pseudo-3D bathymetry 5 m x 5 m, 69 MB, CC BY-NC 4.0); 6618253 (Mahuika crater site raw EM data, CC BY 4.0, raw .all files).
- Zenodo 3518747 Balleny Islands raw multibeam (Antarctic, CC BY 4.0; Ross Dependency area: global report).
- NIWA ArcGIS Online items by individuals (owner GBS_AliciaFC, Massey/Illinois web maps of the 250 m grid): mirrors, no new data.
- Commercial: not searched.

### D: Pacific islands and compilations

- Zenodo: Papua-Woodlark-Solomon (10257561, CC BY 4.0), Hunga (7456324, CC BY 4.0), Rapa Nui (18982431, CC0), Mariana Trough (19689234, CC BY 4.0); IODP Expedition 330 bathymetry (Louisville seamounts, Zenodo 20835810/20835809, CC BY 4.0; title only read); GMT papers on the Kermadec-Tonga trenches (3518972) and the Vityaz and Vanuatu trenches (3794155, 10 MB): analyses, not source grids.
- PANGAEA: SO252 Ritter Island 25 m (929026, CC BY 4.0), SO216 Manus grid (770855, grid behind a 401), raw SONNE EM120/EM122/EM710 sets (row 317, all CC BY 4.0). A bbox search for bathymetry/multibeam titles found nothing for Pitcairn, Easter, the Tuamotu-Marquesas, New Caledonia-Vanuatu, Fiji-Tonga-Samoa-Wallis, Kiribati-Tuvalu-Nauru or the Palau-FSM-Marshalls boxes beyond the SONNE sets and the SO264 Emperor-chain set.
- MGDS/IEDA: Woodlark, Tonga Trench, Lau, Falkor FK171224, Johnston Atoll KM1417 (EM122, CC BY-NC-SA 3.0 US, 10.1594/IEDA/321512), many Melville PNG cruises; licences mostly CC BY-NC-SA.
- SEANOE (Ifremer): rows 309-311. Commercial: none in this area read.

## 5. Unverified (routes tried)

### A: Australia Commonwealth and eastern states

- GA services.ga.gov.au (REST/WMS/WCS for the 2009 grid): CloudFront 403 on three paths. Tried the eCat links, the AusSeabed Warehouse WMS (works) and files.ausseabed.gov.au (works) instead.
- NSW SEED and Planning Portal file downloads (HEAD 202, AWS WAF challenge): licence read from CKAN, files not fetched; alternatives: ELVIS and AODN for the same data.
- DEECA Datashare VCDEM download (login and cart): metadata read via metashare and CKAN; vertical datum of VCDEM not stated in what was read; the Datashare ordering process not exercised.
- GBRMPA GBR10 raw depth zip (the Image Collection's download items f644f02e..., 84afec1c... answer "Item does not exist or is inaccessible"); tile package item readable only as metadata.
- Cell size of the NSW bathymetry mosaic and the DEA Intertidal footprint list; AusENC licence (hydro.gov.au unreachable); the Gold Coast waterway survey formats; the 2009 national grid's cell size in degrees and its use limitation text; the eCat licences of the Tasmanian GA harvested rows (row 61); the QLD "Queensland Globe" bathymetry layers; NCI THREDDS availability of per-survey grids (the S3 bucket works, THREDDS not tried).

### B: WA, SA, NT, external territories, inland

- Lord Howe Island: the vertical datum of the 2008 SS06-2008 survey grids (10 m and 50 m); Record 2010/36 covers the 2010 grids only and the survey's own eCat record was not read.
- Licence per record for the Marine Park grids (row 122), the WA survey grids (row 111), the HIPP records other than SI 1014 (rows 109, 120, 134): only the layer names and search lists were seen.
- AADC (Australian Antarctic Data Centre) search for Heard/Macquarie bathymetry: the JSON endpoint https://data.aad.gov.au/metadata/records/?format=json ignored the search parameters (total stayed 3435); only records fetched by exact id (AADC_Macquarie_bathy_grid) worked. Other Macquarie or Heard AADC grids may exist.
- NW Shelf DEM v2 COG files: eCat links returned HTTP 404 (row 106); the zips were not HEAD-checked beyond the record's size.
- AHO Reference Surfaces licence (row 110): the eCat record has an empty licence field.
- Tasmanian (Hydro Tasmania) lake data: www.tas.gov.au/data presented a Cloudflare challenge ("Just a moment..."); not bypassed; Hydro Tasmania covered only by data.gov.au and eCat queries.
- PANGAEA, SEANOE, MGDS/R2R cruise grids for the Indian Ocean territories and the Southern Ocean around Macquarie/Heard: not queried here.
- Western Australian Water Corporation, Seqwater, WaterNSW, Melbourne Water, SA Water, Hydro Tasmania own portals (not CKAN-listed): not searched directly; their listings on state CKANs returned no depth data.
- Actual file content of the MDB DEM (328.8 GB, over the 5 GB per-source bound): not downloaded; metadata only. The Gunn Point peninsula zip (row 133, https://ftp-dlrm.nt.gov.au/, about 22.8 MB) could not be read: the NT file server ended the transfer early three times (4.9, 9.7 and 11.1 MB received, no valid zip).

### C: New Zealand and its realm

- Gisborne District Council hub: search API 401 (not worked round).
- Bay of Plenty hub domain: not found; BoPRC ArcGIS server root read, no bathymetry service by name.
- LDS grid export without login: not tested.
- Datum of the nz-coastal DEM beyond the Q&A statement (NZVD2016): per-survey metadata carries no datum field.
- Cook Islands Seabed Minerals Authority data: site pages showed no data portal; SPC Pacific Data Hub blocked by Cloudflare challenge; SPREP portal search 404.
- Kermadec, Chatham, Campbell, Niue, Tokelau multibeam: nothing found (see row 281).
- Council lake surveys, NIWA lake chart digital data, hydro lakes: nothing found (row 271).
- GNS Zealandia bathymetry grid download: not found.

### D: Pacific islands and compilations

- Pacific Data Hub and SPC GeoNode/GeoServer (403 challenge, not bypassed; Wayback offline); SPC-held PCRAFI/PACCSAP lidar for Samoa, Tonga, Vanuatu, Kiribati and Tuvalu (all request-only per the terrain report; this run read only the UNDP Tuvalu page).
- Ifremer Coral Sea Park synthesis file URLs (Sextant XML has no link); PANGAEA 770855 licence and grid (401); the MGDS Melville/PNG grids' exact licence strings.
- ISA DeepData terms and any bathymetry layer (static files timed out; terms pages 404).
- Allen Coral Atlas licence. JAMSTEC. Fiji, Solomon, Tonga, Kiribati, Nauru, Tuvalu national offices (hosts not reached or not fetched). Pitcairn Blue Belt multibeam. Shom data for Tuamotu/Marquesas beyond what the WFS counts show.
### Second pass

**Second pass, New Zealand: unverified and accidental**

- "Lake Rotorua 2 m bathymetric grid, free download, max depth 53.5 m off Sulphur Point" and the GNS "Download the map" link: from a web-search summary only; `https://gns.cri.nz/...` answered with an Imperva/Incapsula challenge page (HTTP 200 with a script stub), not worked round; `https://astrobiology.com/?p=23002` returned an image. The grid claim is therefore unsourced here.
- GNS shop PDF files at NZ$0.00 were not downloaded (needs a cart/checkout; not tried).
- BoPRC bathymetry data vintage/source, datum and licence: unstated; the web experience app's "download contours" widget (Contours Export GPServer `https://gis.boprc.govt.nz/server2/rest/services/GP_Services/Contours_Export/GPServer`) not run.
- LINZ Esri org lists a "Wave and Hydrodynamic models" story map and a "Supporting sustainable maritime activities" story map (not read).
- Accidental: the Hawke's Bay 2022 and 2019 LINZ layers' download counts are 47 and 40; layer 116627 72; 116625 87 (shows these are used).
- `catalogue.data.govt.nz` is behind a bot challenge for scripted clients (both HTML and the CKAN API), so LINZ marine inventory records there cannot be read.

**Second pass, Australia: unverified and accidental**

- Unverified: the vertical datum of Sydney Harbour 2004 and 1997 (the register row has none; the GA eCat record was not fetched, services.ga.gov.au answered 403 earlier); what the ELVIS app lists for Victoria (JavaScript app); whether ELVIS delivers Moreton Bay SDB without a login (order by emailed link, not tested); sizes of the 67 NSW OEH GeoTIFFs sum to 0.756 GB (HEAD only; the 231 BTYGRD files include other formats, not summed); the planningportal EPA zip name was truncated in CKAN ("marine_eepa_post_flood_"), the 202 came from the truncated URL as given, so the challenge there is assumed, not shown for a full file name; the Tasmanian 5 m contour records' licence (stubs say "notspecified").
- Accidental: the Wayback availability API gave a 2026-10-26 snapshot of hydro.gov.au, which shows the AHO site was rebuilt (menus include S-100 products and a "ChartScheme 2027" news item); the CKAN list of the LIST's eleven bathymetry records also includes "Aerial survey of giant kelp on the east coast of Tasmania" (not bathymetry). A first attempt to pipe a Python one-liner via the shell was refused by the tool permission layer and redone with script files; scratch is in the agent's own folder only.

**Second pass, Pacific: unverified and accidental**

- Row 506 download: the WCS/WMS at datasuds-geo.ird.fr redirects to maintenance.ird.fr; whether the GeoTIFFs are fetchable is untested. The DataCite year 1001 for the 1:1,000,000 map is a placeholder ("Date of publication: unknown").
- Row 500: NA176 file names, sizes and the GMRT-synthesis share of it were not read; the DataCite box is rounded.
- Row 502 box: DataCite gives west 157.85 E and east -81 W, which is an antimeridian-crossing box expressed with swapped signs; treat it as the equatorial track.
- Row 503/504: SO299 cruise dates in the description read "14.04.2023 - 02.06.2023" for both SO298 and SO299, so one of the two text dates in DataCite is probably copied.
- Row 301 conclusion that no file link exists depends on Sextant/Archimer pages as served today; the Sextant landing page itself (https://sextant.ifremer.fr/record/...) is a script page I did not render.
- ISA map layer list (row 354) unread because main.js timed out.
- Accidental: curl of ~12 hostnames that do not exist (DNS errors only); scratch folder `pscr` under the b1322b scratchpad is mine.



## 6. Anything accidental

### A: Australia Commonwealth and eastern states

- The AusSeabed eCat footprint link names a WFS type (AusSeabed_Bathymetry_Surveys_Coverage) that no longer exists; the live names are MARINEDATAREGISTER_*_INDEX. Several register name strings are mangled: "Australian Bathymetry and Topography Bathymetry ESRI Service not on GeoServer 250m 2009", "AHO Reference Surfaces Lombadina Point Bathymetry Waiting on approval from AHO 0.5m 2023", "Satellite-dervived", "NSW Costal Reforms".
- Licence conflicts: Gold Coast DTM CC BY 2.5 AU (data.gov.au) vs CC BY 4.0 (register); NSW single-beam CC BY 4.0 (AODN) vs unversioned "cc-by" (SEED); data.gov.au harvests report "notspecified" for GA and AODN rows whose source records say CC BY 4.0.
- eCat 2023 national grid has no licence line while the 2009, 2024 and 2026 grids do. The 2024 record says "superseded on 4/8/2026" and the 2023 record "superseded on 5/12/2024"; the register still lists the 2023 and 2024 grids and not the 2026 grid (compilations stop at 2024).
- VCDEM record text calls its 2021 product "VCDEM2022 produced by Veris" in one paragraph and "VCDEM2021" in the others. The eCat HIPP title reads "Approaches to Morton Bay".
- IMAS packaged zips run to 97 G (slope.zip). The register's 1,247 Restricted rows are 38% of 3,295.
- Not in my scope but seen: the register lists Lord Howe Island DEM bathymetry 8-250 m 2010 (agent B), Cocos and Christmas Island SDB 2 m 2022 (agent B), Perth 30 m 2026 grid (agent B).

### B: WA, SA, NT, external territories, inland

- Norfolk record 97136a6d (1 m multibeam) contains a copied HIPP paragraph about a "30 metre Shoal Depth True Position (SDTP) surface" and "Full resolution surfaces available from AHO on request": the 30 m text may not describe the 1 m file offered. Same pattern as the AHO records.
- The Darwin 1 m grid: eCat says CC BY 4.0; the Metadata.txt inside the metadata zip says CC BY 3.0 and "(C) Commonwealth of Australia (Geoscience Australia) 2019".
- Size mismatches: Kimberley SDB zip says 244.3 MB in the record, HEAD gives 296,722,104 bytes; Shark Bay says 587.6 MB, HEAD 405,093,458; Norfolk says 394.9 MB, HEAD 414,129,802; Cocos 2022 says 147.4 MB, HEAD 154,584,010 (probably MB vs MiB, but the first two differ the other way).
- The AusSeabed WFS GetCapabilities lists 560 feature types but the layer `AusSeabed_Bathymetry_Surveys_Coverage` named in the eCat record is "unknown" to the server (exception text: "Feature type :AusSeabed_Bathymetry_Surveys_Coverage unknown"); use the per-product layers listed in GetCapabilities instead.
- data.gov.au lists GA and CSIRO items as licence "notspecified" even where the source says CC BY 4.0 or CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 (rows 170, 150).
- The CSIRO MDB DEM collection has an ArcGIS .xml whose item location is a CSIRO UNC path (`\\fs1-cbr.nexus.csiro.au\...`): harmless, but the record exposes internal paths.
- The NT Darwin package zip on ftp-dlrm is 4.4 MB, far smaller than the 3.5 GB GA grid.

### C: New Zealand and its realm

- LINZ hydrographic page (data "on request") versus LDS API (64 downloadable CC BY 4.0 grids): both fetched; the API is newer. The index layer 122710 still says models are ordered by email. Whether the LDS web "Download" works anonymously is untested.
- NIWA: 250 m grid licence appears as "free" (download page), "All rights reserved" (further-information page), CC BY-NC 4.0 (ArcGIS item) and NODL BY-NN-NC-SA (licence page, Wayback 2022). The ArcGIS 250 m service is password-protected since 07/2025 per a mirror item. Only the 2008 CANZ zips were confirmed downloadable (HEAD 200).
- Item 3DCM programme polygon service is named `3DCM_Areas_Rev_Feb2026` but the layer inside is `3DCM_Areas_Rev_Aug2026` and the item title says Sep 2026 (updated 15 Sep 2026).
- LDS layer 116724 and 116614 show `published_at` 2026-07 though sitting in the 2024-09 batch: re-published.
- Datum split: lidar NZVD2016 vs multibeam approximate LAT vs Marlborough NZVD2009 and ECan NZVD2016.
- Environment Canterbury item URL is a `utility.arcgis.com/usrsvcs/servers/...` proxy, not an ECan host.
- The terrain report said the LDS pages are a JS app: confirmed, but the Koordinates API route works anonymously for metadata.

### D: Pacific islands and compilations

- data.gouv.fr's Clipperton 20 m link is `.../file.MNT_COTIER_...7z` and gives 404; `.../file/MNT_COTIER_...7z` gives 200 (9.1 MB).
- The first ScienceBase item I opened for Majuro (5ab2ac7ce4b081f61ab4613b) is an inundation project page, not the data; the data release is 59557881e4b04e08be532c9a.
- The NOAA TID service is named and labelled GEBCO_2024; the Manus Basin point shows predicted (40) although PANGAEA 770855 has a 35 m grid there.
- PangAEA's SO216 "other version" grid answers 401 although the record is public; DataCite has no record for 10.1594/PANGAEA.770855.
- My Fiji NCEI box stops at 180 E, so its 39 undercounts surveys east of the antimeridian. The CCZ NCEI box includes Hawaii.
- A web search for Pitcairn Blue Belt returned only Tristan da Cunha; search-result text was not used as evidence.

## 7. Claims of the earlier survey (RESEARCH-BATHYMETRY.md) for this region

### A: Australia Commonwealth and eastern states

- IMAS/Seamap composite, 10 m to 200 m depth up to 210 m, 32/64/128 m on the slope, CC BY 4.0, MSL EPSG::5715, WMS, footprint WFS, unzipped packages may exceed 100 GB: CONFIRMED from the IMAS GeoNetwork record. "COG GeoTIFF": NOT CONFIRMED: files are tiled BigTIFF GeoTIFF (LZW, 512 px tiles); overviews unchecked.
- GA 250 m 2023 grid, licence line names only the Commonwealth copyright: CONFIRMED (the 2023 record has no Creative Commons constraint). "Superseded": CONFIRMED and updated: 2024 (CC BY 4.0) and 2026 (CC BY 4.0) grids exist. "Not for navigation": 2023 record text not checked; the 2024 and 2026 records and the register say so.
- "0.0025 degrees, 2.6 GB zip": 2.6 GB CONFIRMED; the degree spacing NOT CONFIRMED (the 2023 record says 250 m).
- NSW multibeam "CC BY 3.0 AU": CONTRADICTED: CKAN and SEED say "cc-by" without version; AODN single-beam says CC BY 4.0.
- "Victoria/Tasmania: search only": now CONFIRMED with records for Victoria (VCDEM 2021, nearshore 20 m, VFA contours) and Tasmania (SeaMap, LIST, Storm Bay).
- The brief's "Victorian Coastal DEM 2017 2.5/10 m": the current product is VCDEM 2021 (2.5 m and 10 m); the 2017 and 2010 versions are named as predecessors in the record.
- GA 5 m multibeam "50 m multibeam compilation": CONFIRMED as 50 m, 2018, CC BY 4.0 (5.6 GB).
- Hawaii HMRG 50 m: not in my area.

### B: WA, SA, NT, external territories, inland

- "Australia: IMAS/Seamap multi-resolution composite (2026), 10 m to 200 m depth ... CC BY 4.0": confirmed in part (IMAS record fetched: zone grids 10, 32, 64, 128, 210 m; CC BY 4.0 link present; it names the WA Bathymetry Portal among its inputs). The "unzipped packages may exceed 100 GB" figure was not tested. This is a national row for agent A.
- "GA 250 m 2023 grid ... superseded": confirmed: eCat 7ff1f558 (AusBathyTopo 250m 2026, published 2026-08-04) says it replaces the 2024 version; the 2023 record 00648206 is still listed; the 2026 grid excludes HIMI and AAT and includes Macquarie, Norfolk, Christmas, Cocos.
- "GA 2023 record names no open licence" (earlier survey, row 24): contradicted for the 2026 record, whose legal constraint is "Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International Licence"; the 2023 record's wording was not re-read.
- "Australian states: nothing verified beyond this": confirmed for that survey; this report supplies WA, SA and NT (section 2.3).
- "Antarctic IBCSO v2": not in my area (pointer, row 151).

### C: New Zealand and its realm

- RESEARCH-BATHYMETRY.md: "New Zealand ... no verified national open product": contradicted (rows 205, 209, 212-214).
- "NIWA 250 m NZ grid and its licence (conflicting in search results)": resolved as above (rows 200-201); the grid is non-commercial (CC BY-NC / BY-NN-NC-SA).
- "NIWA download page fetch returned nothing usable": confirmed for the live page (403); Wayback copy works.
- terrain-rest.md: "nz-coastal: 1 m topobathy DEM, seabed to about 25 m depth, CC BY 4.0, S3/STAC": confirmed; 2 m and 10 m versions exist per survey; additional facts in row 205.
- "LDS layer pages are a Koordinates JavaScript app": confirmed; the `/services/api/v1.x/layers/` API is open for metadata.
- "data.govt.nz catalogue answered a bot-challenge page": confirmed (CKAN API); not bypassed.
- "Regional councils need no row of their own: their lidar is inside the collection list": confirmed for land lidar; for bathymetry council rows are in 2.7.
- Fiordland/Chatham/Kermadec/Auckland/Campbell outside the 1 m land DEM polygon: not re-tested here; for bathymetry the LDS depth models do cover Fiordland, Auckland Islands, Bounty/Antipodes and Snares.

### D: Pacific islands and compilations

- "SHOM HOMONIM ~100 m MNT, Licence Ouverte 2.0" (in the Pacific rows, then unverified): confirmed for Pacific façade products (Licence Ouverte 2.0 via data.gouv.fr, both PBMA and NM datums offered; rows 300, 302-305). The "services.data.shom.fr record 403" was not reproduced; data.gouv.fr and the download services answer 200.
- "Not even searched: Pacific island states": now searched; result above.
- CUDEM listing for Hawaii, Guam, American Samoa and CNMI (section 3): not checked here; the PacIOOS ids and NAMERICA rows are consistent with PIBHMC/NCEI grids for those places.
- NIWA 250 m NZ grid (unverified there): not part of my area; https://niwa.co.nz/our-science/oceans/bathymetry answered 404.
- GEBCO regional project list (IBCSEP etc.): not checked here.
